{**conductor_manifest(\"built_in.gamma\"), \"kind\": \"built_in\"},\n+            ],\n+            node_registry=self.node_registry,\n+        )\n+\n+        registry.validate_all()\n+\n+        self.assertEqual(registry.get(\"external.alpha\").child_nodes, (\"builtin.transcribe\",))\n+        self.assertEqual([item.id for item in registry.list()], [\"built_in.gamma\", \"external.alpha\", \"external.beta\"])\n+        self.assertEqual([item.id for item in registry.list(kind=\"external\")], [\"external.alpha\", \"external.beta\"])\n+        exported = registry.to_dict(kind=\"built_in\")\n+        self.assertEqual([item[\"id\"] for item in exported[\"conductors\"]], [\"built_in.gamma\"])\n+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(registry.to_json(kind=\"built_in\"))[\"conductors\"][0][\"id\"], \"built_in.gamma\")\n+        self.assertIn(\"external.alpha\", registry.as_mapping())\n+\n+    def test_invalid_kind_filter_is_rejected(self) -> None:\n+        registry = ConductorRegistry(node_registry=self.node_registry)\n+\n+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ConductorRegistryError, \"kind must be\"):\n+            registry.list(kind=\"custom\")\n+\n+    def test_duplicate_ids_are_rejected_at_registration(self) -> None:\n+        registry = ConductorRegistry([conductor_manifest(\"external.duplicate\")], node_registry=self.node_registry)\n+\n+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ConductorRegistryError, \"duplicate conductor id\"):\n+            registry.register(conductor_manifest(\"external.duplicate\"))\n+\n+    def test_unknown_child_node_is_rejected(self) -> None:\n+        registry = ConductorRegistry(\n+            [conductor_manifest(\"external.bad_node\", child_nodes=[\"builtin.missing\"])],\n+            node_registry=self.node_registry,\n+        )\n+\n+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ConductorRegistryError, \"unknown child node\"):\n+            registry.validate_all()\n+\n+    def test_unknown_child_conductor_is_rejected(self) -> None:\n+        registry = ConductorRegistry(\n+            [conductor_manifest(\"external.parent\", child_conductors=[\"external.missing\"])],\n+            node_registry=self.node_registry,\n+        )\n+\n+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ConductorRegistryError, \"unknown child conductor\"):\n+            registry.validate_all()\n+\n+    def test_direct_self_reference_is_rejected(self) -> None:\n+        registry = ConductorRegistry(\n+            [conductor_manifest(\"external.self\", child_conductors=[\"external.self\"])],\n+            node_registry=self.node_registry,\n+        )\n+\n+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ConductorRegistryError, \"cannot reference itself\"):\n+            registry.validate_all()\n+\n+    def test_indirect_cycle_is_rejected(self) -> None:\n+        registry = ConductorRegistry(\n+            [\n+                conductor_manifest(\"external.alpha\", child_conductors=[\"external.beta\"]),\n+                conductor_manifest(\"external.beta\", child_conductors=[\"external.gamma\"]),\n+                conductor_manifest(\"external.gamma\", child_conductors=[\"external.alpha\"]),\n+            ],\n+            node_registry=self.node_registry,\n+        )\n+\n+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ConductorRegistryError, \"conductor cycle detected\"):\n+            registry.validate_all()\n+\n+    def test_curated_folder_conductors_load_through_registry(self) -> None:\n+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n+            root = Path(tmp)\n+            conductor_root = root / \"example\"\n+            conductor_root.mkdir()\n+            (conductor_root / \"conductor.py\").write_text(\n+                \"\\n\".join(\n+                    [\n+                        \"from return argv\n+\n+\n+def _planned_steps(args) -> list[pipeline.Step]:\n+    skipped = set(args.skip)\n+    steps = [step for step in pipeline.build_steps(args) if step.name not in skipped]\n+    if If packaging metadata is added later, include `artagents/nodes/curated/**` as package data so installed distributions can discover the same folder nodes.\n \n+## Conductors\n+\n+Nodes execute one unit of work. Conductors orchestrate nodes and may call declared child conductors; files under `artagents/nodes/` must not import or call `artagents.conductors`.\n+\n+`pipeline.py conductors ...` exposes orchestrators as first-class metadata and run targets while preserving the existing root launchers. The legacy commands below still work:\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py --video SRC.mp4 --brief brief.txt --out runs/example --render\n+python3 event_talks.py render --manifest runs/event/talks.json --out-dir runs/event/rendered\n+```\n+\n+Conductor discovery and validation:\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors list\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors list --json --kind built_in\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors inspect builtin.hype --json\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors validate\n+```\n+\n+Dry-run orchestrators without executing their underlying pipeline:\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors run builtin.hype --out runs/example --brief brief.txt --dry-run -- --target-duration 12 --from cut\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors run builtin.event_talks --out runs/event --dry-run -- ados-sunday-template --out runs/event/talks.json\n+```\n+\n ## Pure-generative and no-audio modes\n \n `pipeline.py` also supports an audio-backed no-video path:\n*** Update File: SKILL.md\n@@\n Event talk splitting:\n \n ```bash\n python3 transcribe.py --audio talk.wav --out runs/event/transcript --env-file /path/to/.env\n@@\n python3 event_talks.py render --manifest runs/event/talks.json --out-dir runs/event/rendered\n ```\n \n+Conductors expose orchestrators alongside nodes. Nodes execute one unit; conductors run nodes and may call declared child conductors. Do not import or call `artagents.conductors` from files under `artagents/nodes/`.\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors list\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors inspect builtin.hype --json\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors validate\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors run builtin.hype --out runs/<name> --brief brief.txt --dry-run -- --target-duration 12 --from cut\n+python3 pipeline.py conductors run builtin.event_talks --out runs/event --dry-run -- ados-sunday-template --out runs/event/talks.json\n+```\n+\n+The legacy root launchers remain supported:\n+\n+```bash\n+python3 pipeline.py --video SRC.mp4 --brief brief.txt --out runs/<name> --render\n+python3 event_talks.py render --manifest runs/event/talks.json --out-dir runs/event/rendered\n+```\n+\n For polished long event-talk videos, keep `event_talks.py render` on its default `--renderer remotion-wrapper`: Remotion renders the animated intro/outro cards, while ffmpeg handles the long media pass, lower-third, corner logo, and final concat. Card renders are cached beside the card MP4s and are invalidated by talk metadata, dimensions, duration, and brand asset mtimes; use `--force-card-render` when iterating on card animation code. Full `--renderer remotion` writes Reigh-style timeline/assets JSON and renders the whole talk through `render_remotion.py`, but it can be slow and disk-heavy for 10+ minute talks and now preflights free disk. `--renderer ffmpeg-proof` is only for quick boundary/proof checks, not final branded output.\n@@\n ## Important Contracts\n \n - `pipeline.py --brief ...` is the canonical top-level flow.\n+- Nodes execute one unit; conductors orchestrate nodes and may call declared child conductors. Nodes must not call conductors.\n - `cut.py` emits `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, `hype.metadata.json`, and `hype.edl.csv`.\n*** End Patch\n"
